1b1('dul ba): @##//'dul ba rnam par 'byed pa/bam po bzhi bcu rtsa gsum pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab na 'od ma'i tshal ka lan da ka'i gnas na bzhugs so//
1b2('dul ba): gang gi tshe tshe dang ldan pa gyad bu nor dge 'dun gyis gnas ma' 'bog pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos pa'i tshe na dge slong mdza' bo dang /sa las skyes gnyis tshe dang ldan pa gyad bu no ra 
1b3('dul ba): dang tshe rabs tshe rabs nas khon yod pas dge slong mdza' bo dang /sa las skyes gnyis tshe dang lngan pa nye dga' dang lhan cig tu 'du 'dzi'i gtam gyis 'khod pa na/tshe dang ldan pa gyad 
1b4('dul ba): bu nor phyogs der 'ongs pa dang /de gnyis kyis nye dga' la smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa nye dga' gal te khyod kyang dge 'dun gyis gnas mal 'bog pa dang /zas las sko bar bskos 
1b5('dul ba): par gyur na/khyod kyang kho bo cag la 'di lta ste/dper na tshe dang ldan pa gyad bu nong bzhin du mtho 'tsham par 'gyur ro//nye dga' sa de gnyis la smras pa/tshe dang ldan ba gnyis 
2a1('dul ba): @#//khyed gnyis dge 'dun mthun pas dge slong gang gnas mal 'phog pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos par shes bzhin du de la ngor 'phya'am/de gnyis kyis smras pa/ci kho bo cag gnyis 'dur yang mi dbang ngam/dge slong don nyung du 
2a2('dul ba): dag ci'i phyir dge slong dag dge 'dun mthun pas dge/slong gang gnas mal 'bog pa dang /zas la skyo bar bskos par shes bzhin/de la ngor 'phya bar byed ces smod par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong 
2a3('dul ba): rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag khyed kyis dge slong mdza' bo dang /sa las skyes gnyis sam/gzhan yang de lta bu gang yin pa de ngor 'phya ba'i grangs su chug shig/
2a4('dul ba): 'di ltar grangs su gzhug par bya ste/gnas mal bsham pa byas nas/gaN+DI brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge slong rnams la yang dag par bsko nas dge 'dun thams cad 'dug cing mthun par gyur pa la dge slong gcig gis gsol ba bya ste/
2a5('dul ba): dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/dge slong mdza' bo dang /sa las skyes 'di gnyis 'tshal bzhin du dge slong gang dge 'dun mthun pas gnas mal 'bog pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos pa tshe dang ldan pa gyad bu nor de la ngor 'phyan/
2a6('dul ba): gal te dge 'dun gyi dus la bab cing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang bar mdzod cig dang /dge 'dun gyis dge slong mdza' bo dang sa las skyes ngor 'phya ba'i grangs su stsal to//'di ni gsol ba'o//gang gi tshe dge 'dun gyis dge slong 
2b1('dul ba): mdza' bo dang /sa las skyes gnyis ngor 'phya ba'i grangs su bcug pa de'i tshe na de gnyis tshe dang ldan pa nye dga' dang lhan cig gtam zer zhing 'dug pa na/tshe dang ldan pa gyad bu nor phyogs der 'ongs pa dang /de gnyis kyis nye dga' la smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa nye dag 'gal te khyod kyang dge 
2b2('dul ba): 'dun gyis gnas mal 'bog pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos par gyur na/khyod kyang kho bo cag la 'di lta ste dper na kha cig bzhin du mtho 'tsham par 'gyur ro//nye dga' sa de gnyis la smras pa/tshe dang ldan ba gnyis khyed gnyis dge 'dun mthun pas dge slong gang gnas mal 'bog 
2b3('dul ba): pa dang /zas la sbo bar bskos par shes bzhin du de la gzhogs 'phyas byed dam/de gnyis kyis smras pa/chi kho bo cag gnyis kyis 'ga' zhig gi ming dam rus nas smos sam/nye dga' sa smras pa/gal te ming dang rus ma smos par gzhogs 'phyas byed du 
2b4('dul ba): gnang na/nye dga' sa kyang 'jig rten thams cad la ming dang rus mi smos par gzhogs 'phyas byas par 'gyur ro//dag slong don nyung du dag ci'i phyir dge slong dag dge 'dun mthun pas/dge slong gang gnas mal 'phog pa dang /zas la sko par bskos par shes bzhin 
2b5('dul ba): de la gzhogs 'phyas byed ces smod par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag khyed kyis dge slong mdza' bo dang /sa las skyes gnyis 
2b6('dul ba): sam/gzhan yang de lta bu gang yin pa de gzhogs 'phyas byed pa'i grangs su chug shig 'di ltar grangs su gzhug par bya ste/gnas mal bsham pa byas nas gaN+DI brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge slong rnams la yang dag par bsgo nas dge 'dun thams cad 'dug cing mthun 
2b7('dul ba): par gyur pa la dge slong gcig gis gsol ba bya ste/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/dge slong mdza' bo dang /se las skyes 'di gnyis 'tshal bzhin du dge slong gang dge 'dun mthun pas gnas mal 'bog pa dang /zas la sko bar bskos pa tshe dang ldan pa

3b1('dul ba): bar gyi ste zhes bya ba la sogs pa snga ma bzhin no//nga'i nyan thos rnams kyis 'dul ba la bslab pa'i gzhi 'di ltar gdon par bya ste/'phya'am gzhogs 'phyas byed na ltung byed do//'phya'am gzhogs 'phyas byedan zhes bya ba ni 'phya ba dang gzhogs 'phyas bya ba'i bsam 
3b2('dul ba): pas dag dang /tshig dang /tha snyad dang /skad dang /rjod pa dang /nges pa'i tshig dang /tshig gi lam dang /tshig gi sgra dang /ngag gi lam dang /ngag gi rnam par rig byed rjod pa'o//ltung byed do zhes bya ba ni sems can dmyal ba dang /dud 'gro dang /yi dgas dang /ngan song 
3b3('dul ba): dang /ngan 'gro dang /log par ltung ba dag tu sreg par byed/'tshod par byed/ltung bar byed pa'o//gzhan yang ji srid du ltung ba de ma bshags shing so sor ma bshags na dge ba'i chos rnams la sgrib par byed pa ste/des na ltung byed do zhes gsungs so//de la ltung 
3b4('dul ba): bar ji ltar 'gyur zhe na/dge slong dge 'dun gyis bgrangs shing /de yang gang zag bcu gnyis su bskos pa sbyor ba ma btang ba dag la 'phyan ltung byed du 'gyur ro//gzhogs 'phyas byed na ltung byed du 'gyur ro/'phya ba dang gzhogs 'phyas byed na ltud byed du 'gyur ro//'phya ba dang gzhogs 'phyis byed na ltung byed du 
3b5('dul ba): 'gyur ro//dge slong dge 'dun gyis grangs shing de yang gang zag bcu gnyissu bskos pa sbyor ba btang ba dag la 'phya na ltung byed du 'gyur lo//gzhogs 'phyis byed na ltud byed du gyur ro//'phya ba dang gzhigs 'phyis byed na ltung bye da du 'gyur ro//dge slong dge bdun gyis ma bgrangs shing de yang gang 
3b6('dul ba): bgrangs shing de yang gang zagabacu gnyis su ma bskos pa sbyor ba ma btad pa dag la 'phya na ltung bye da du 'gyur/gzhogs 'phyis kyi ngan ltung byed du 'gyur ro //'phya ba dang gzhigs 'phyas byed na ltung ba dang /de yang gang zag bcu gnyi sum skos 
3b7('dul ba): zag bcu gnyis su ma bskos pasbyor ba btang ba dag la phyan ltung byed du 'gyur ro//gzhogs 'phyis byed ni ltung byed du 'gyur ro/'phyi ba dang gzhigs 'phyis bye da na ltung byed du 'gyur ro//dge slong dge 'dun gyis ma bgrangs shing /de yang gang zag bcuganyis sum skos
4a1('dul ba): @#//pa spyod pa ma btang ba dag la 'phyan nyes byas su 'gyur ro//gzhogs 'phyas byed na nyes byas su 'gyur ro//'phya ba dang gzhogs 'phyas byed na nyesabyas su 'gyur ro//dge slong dge 'dun gyis ma bgrangs shing de yang gang zag bcu gnyis su bskos pa sbyor 
4a2('dul ba): ba btang ba dag la 'phya na nyes byas su 'gyur ro/gzhogs 'phyas byed na nyes
4a3('dul ba):  byas su 'gyur ro//gzhon 'phyas byed na nyes byas sa 'gyur ro//'phya ba dang gzhogs 'phya byed na nyes byas su 'gyu ro//dge slong dge 'dug gyis ma bgrangs shing de lang gang zag bcu gnyis sam bskos pa sbyor ba btang ba dag la 'phyan zhes byas su 
4a4('dul ba): 'gyur ro//gzhogs 'phyis byed na nyes byas su 'a gyur ro//'phya ba dang gzhigs 'phyis byed na nyes bya sa su 'gyur ro//las dang po pa dang /smyos pa dang /sems 'khrugs pa dang /tshor ba dag gis nyen pa la ltung ba med do//'phya ba'i ltung byed de bcu gnyis pa rdzogs 
4a5('dul ba): ston//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kau shA mrI na gdangs can gyi kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so//de'i tshe na tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa ltung ba chos bzhin du phyir mi byed nas/de la dge slong don 'dod pa/bde ba 'dod pa/bde ba la reg par 'dod pa/grub pa dang bde ba 
4a6('dul ba): 'dod pa rnams kyis de la smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa lhung ba byung ba chos bzhin du phyir gyis shig/de ba smras pa/su la ltung ba byung ba de phyir byed par 'gyur ro//khyod la ltung ba byung ba su zhig phyir byed par 'gyur/su 'gyod par 'gyur ba'o//khyod la ltung ba byungab la su 
4a7('dul ba): 'gyod par 'gyur/gal te tshe dang ldan pa khyed cag la ltung ba byung na kho bo'i mdun du ma 'chags par gzhan gyi mdun du shogs shig/tshe dang ldan pa dag khyed cag ni rigs sna tshogs dang /rum sna tshogs dang /khyim sna tshogs nas rab tu byung ba dag yin te/'di lta ste dper na 
4b1('dul ba): shing ljon pa dag gi lo ma sna tshogs rlung gis blags pa dag gcig tu ltung zhing gcig tu 'dug pa de bzhin du khyed cag kyang rigs sna tshogs dang /rus sna tshogs dang /khyim sna tshogs nas rab tu byung ba dag yin gyi/kho bo'i 'phags pas ni byang chub thugs su chud pa yin no zhes 
4b2('dul ba): de dge slong rnams kyis bsgo ba rnal gzon par byed nas/dge slong don nyung ngu dag ci'i phyir dge slong dag dge slong rnams kyis bsgo pa rnal gzon par byed ces smod par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol 
4b3('dul ba): pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag khyed kyis dge slong 'dun pa dang gzhan yang de lta bu gang yin pa la bsgo ba rnal gzon pa'i grangs su chug shig/'di ltar grangs su gzhug par bya ste/gnas mal bsham pa byas nas gaN+DI brdungs te dris pa'i 
4b4('dul ba): tshig gis dge slong rnams la yang dag par bsgo nas/dge 'dun thams cad 'dug cing mthun par gyur pa la dge slong gcig gis gsol ba bya ste/dge 'dun btsun pa rnams gsan du gsol/tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa 'di ltung ba byung yang chos bzhin du slar mi bgyid 
4b5('dul ba): de/'di la dge slong don 'tshal ba/phan pa 'tshal ba/bde ba 'tshal ba/bde ba la reg par 'tshal ba/grub pa dang bde ba 'tshal ba rnams kyis gzhi des glengs kyang dge slong rnams kyis bsgo ba rnal gzon na/gal te dge 'dun gyi dus la ba bcing bzod na dge 'dun gyis gnang 
4b6('dul ba): bar mdzod cig dang /dge 'dun gyis tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa bsgo ba rnal gzon pa'i gangs su stsal to//'di ni gsol pa'o//gang gi tshe na tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa sgo ba rnal gzon pa'i grangs su bcug pa de'i tshe na des bsams pa/bdag la nyes pa gang byung ba de ni bdag 
4b7('dul ba): gis de dag la tshig smras pa las gyur pa yin te/gang gis/skyes bu mkhas dang ldan pa yis//ngag rnams bzhi po bsrung bya ste//nags kyi nang du gong ma sreg/sgra 'byin pas ni zin la ltos//zhes smras pa ni legs par smras pa yin no snyam nas/
5a1('dul ba): @#//
5b1('dul ba): 'di la dge slong gi dge 'dun bsdu ba mdzad do zhes bya ba la sogs pa snga ma bzhin no//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mkhyen bzhin du rmed par mdzad pa ste zhes bya ba la sogs pa yang snga ma bzhin te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasakyis dge slong 'dun pa la 
5b2('dul ba): rmas pa/dge slong khyod dge slong rnams kyis bsgo ba rnal gzon pa dang /cang mi smra bas rna la gzon pa bden nam/btsun pa mad do//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas kyis dge sbyong gi tshul ma yin pa/rjes su mthun pa ma yin pa/rung ba ma yin pa/tshul dang 
5b3('dul ba): mthun pa ma yin pa de ni rab tu byung bas bya ba ma yin no zhes rnam par smad pa mdzad do//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rnam grangs du mas rnam par smad nas/dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag de lta bas na phan yon bcu yang dag par gzigs pas 
5b4('dul ba): 'dul ba la nyan thos rnams kyi bslab pa'i gzhi bca' bar bya ste zhes bya ba la sogs ba snga ma bzhin no//nga'i nyan thos rnams kyis 'dul ba la bslab pa'i gzhi 'di ltar gdon par bya ste/bsgo ba rnal gzon na ltung byed do//bsgo ba rnal gzon na zhes bya ba ni bsgo ba rnal gzon 
5b5('dul ba): pa'i bsam pas ngag dang /tshig dang /tha snyad dang /skad dang /rjod pa dang /nges pa'i tshig dang /tshig gi lam dang /tshig gi sgra dang /ngag gi las dang /ngag gi rnam par rig byed brjod pa'o//ltung byed do zhes bya ba ni sems can dmyal ba dang /dud

6a1('dul ba): @#//pa dang /zhun mar gyi lhung bzed dam/'bru mar gyi lhung bzed dam/sbrang rtsi'i lhung bzed dam/bu ram gyi dbu ba'i lhung bzed kyis 'ong ba mthong ngam zhes zer ba la/des 'di skad ces tshe dang ldan pa kho bos dge slong skra dang kha spu bregs nas ring po ma lon pa 
6a2('dul ba): snam sbyar dmar ser dang lhung bzed zha nye'i dbyar 'dab kyis sbyar ba dang /kha rab spu bus dkris pa dang /zhun mar gyi lhungabazed dam/'bru mar gyi ltung bzed dam/sbrang rtsi'i ltung bzed dam/bu ram gyis dbu ba'i lhung bzed kyi 'ong ba mthong ngam zhes ser ba la/des 'di skad ches tshe dang ldan pa kho bos/dge slong skra dang kha spu begs nas ring po ma lon ba 
6a3('dul ba): dge slong rkang pa gnyis kyis 'ong ba mthong ngo zhes smras na ltung byed du 'gyur ro//dge slong dge slong gi gan du 'ongs te/'di skad ces tshe dang ldan ba khyod kyis dge slong ma sgra bregs nas ring po ma yon bsnam sbyar dmar ser dang lhad bzed zha nye'i dbyar 'dapa kyis sbyar ba dang /
6a4('dul ba): zhun mar gyi lhung bzed dam/'bru mar gyis lhung bzed dam/sbrang rtsi'i lhung bzed dam/bu ram gyi dbu ba'i lhung bzed kyis 'ong ba mthong ngam zhes zer ba la/des 'di skad ces tshe dang ldanapa kho bos dge slong ma skra bregs nas ring po ma lonapa snam sbyar dmar ser dang lhung bzed

6a6('dul ba): byed du 'gyur ro//dge slong ma dang dge tshul dang /dge tshul ma la yang de bzhin du ci rigs par sbyar ro//dge slong dge slong gi gan du 'ongs te/'di skad ces tshe dang ldan pa ji ltar khyim pa bu ram shing gi khur gyis sam/smyig ma'i khur gyis sam/'dam bu'i khur gyis 
6a7('dul ba): sam/shing gi khur gyis sam/zhun mar gyi bum pas sam/'bru mar gyi bum pas sam/sbrang rtsi'i bum pas sam/bu ram gyi dbu ba'i bum pas 'ong ba mthong ngam zhes zer ba la/des 'di skad ces tshe dang ldanapa kho bos khyim pa bu ram shing gi bur gyis sam/smyig 
6b1('dul ba): ma'i khur gyis sam/'dam bu'i khur gyis sam/shing gi khur gyis sam/zhun mar gyi bum pas sam/'bru mar gyi bum pas sam/sbrang rtsi'i bum pas sam/bu ram gyi dbu ba'i bum pas 'ongs pa ni ma mthong gi/'di ltar kho bos khyim pa rkang pa gnyis kyis 
6b2('dul ba): 'ong ba mthong ngo zhes smras na ltung byed du 'gyur ro//dge slong dge slong gi gan du 'ongs te/'di skad ces tshe dang ldan pa ji ltar khyim pa mo gos thams cad sngon pos sam/thams cad ser pos sam/thams cad dmar pos sam/thams cad bdakar pos sam/zhun mar 
6b3('dul ba): gyi bum pas sam/'bru dkar pos sam/sbrang rtsi'i bum pa sam/bu ram gyi dbu ba'ibum pas 'ong ba mthong ngam zhes zer ba la/des 'di skad ces tshe dang ldanapa kho bos khyim pa mo gos thams chad sngon pos sam/thams chad ser pos sam/thams 
6b4('dul ba): cad dmar pos sam/thamasacad dkar pos sam/zhun mar gyi bum bas sam/'bru mar gyi bumapas sam/sbrang rtsi'i bumapas sam/bu ram gyi dbuba'i bum pas 'ong ba ni ma mthong gi/'di lta kho bos khyim pa mo rkang pa gnyis kyis 'ong ba mthong ngo zhes smras 
6b5('dul ba): na ltung byed du 'gyur ro//dge slong gis ji tsam du gzhan 'dri ba la lan gzhan 'debs na thams cad du ltung byed du 'gyur ro//rnam grangs de dag thams cad la cang mi smra bas rna la gzon na nyes byas su 'gyur ro//gshed ma'i gsad par bya ba bros nas de dge slong gi gan du 'ongs 
6b6('dul ba): te 'phags pa ji ltar gsad par bya ba mthong ngam zhes 'dri na/gal te grang ba'i dus yin na dge slong gis gshed ma la bzhin bzangs me khang du zhugs la me 'de shig ces brjod par bya'o//gal te tsha ba'i dus yin na bzhin bzangs je dpong sa'i khang par zhugs shig/
6b7('dul ba): ji skom 'thung shig/je ngal so shig ces brjod par bya'o//gal te des de skad ces bdag mi grang ngo //bdag mi cha'o//bdag mi skom mo//bdag ma chad do//'di ltar bdag gsad par bya ba 'dri'o zhes zer na/de sen mo la bltas te sen mo mthong ngo zhes brjod 
7a1('dul ba): @#//par bya'o//nam mkha' la bltas te nam mkha' mthong ngo zhes brjod par bya'o//gal te 'di skad ces bdag sen mo mi 'dri'o//bdag nam mkha' mi 'dri'o//'di ltar bdag gsad par bya ba 'dri'o zhes zer na/dge slong des phyogs bzhir 
7a2('dul ba): rnam par bltas te 'du byed thams cad bdag med pa yid la byas nas don dam par na sems can ma mthong ngo zhes brjod par bye ba la ltung ba med do//kha na ba dang /khas sman 'dzin pa la ltung ba med do//de nas dang po ba dang /smyon pa dang /sems 'khrugs pa dang /tshor ba dag 
7a3('dul ba): gis nyen pa la ltung ba med do////sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la dge slong rnams kyi de zhes pa/btsun pa tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa tshangs pa mtshungs par spyod pa rnams kyi ngag mi nyan nas bzhi can la gnon pa dang /cang mi smra bas rnal gzon pa'i grangs su stsal ba la 
7a4('dul ba): gzigs/dge slong dag da ltar du ma zad kyi 'di ltar 'das pa'i dus na yang 'di dge ba'i bshes gnyen rnams kyi ngag mi nyan na sa tshul ma yin pas sdug bsngal bar gyur te/de nyon cig/dge slong dag sngon bya dra ba dran bya ba'i dus ni rje bzhi gan ru sa sbal gcig dang dang pa gnyis 
7a5('dul ba): shig 'khod de/de nas dus gzhan zhig na lo bcu gnyis su than pa byung nas des rdzing de bskam par brtsams so//chos nyid kyis na 'jig rten gnas pa chags nas ring po ma lon pa'i tshe na dud 'gro dag kyang smra zhes pas ngang pa gnyis kyis rus sbal la smras pa/grogs po 
7a6('dul ba): kho bo cag gnyis ni rdzing gzhan du 'gro'o//rus sbal gyis smras pa/grogs po gnyis kho bo yang khrid de song shig/ngang pa gnyis kyis smras pa/khyod ji ltar khrid de 'gro bar bya/rus sbal gyis smras pa/kho bo shing gi dbus su khas gzung bar byas/khyed gnyis kyis rtse mo 
7a7('dul ba): gnyis nas zung la deng shig/ngang pa gnyis kyi sa smras pa/khyod smra 'dod pa yin pas tshig rung mi nus kyis kho bo cag gnyis khyer te mi 'gro'o//rus sbal gyis smra ba/tshig bsrung bar bya'o//ngang pa gnyis kyis rus sba la de khyer te steng gi nam mkha' la 'phags nas 
7b1('dul ba): ji tsam na ri brags zhig gi steng na yar song ba dang /khye'u dang bu mo dag gis mthong nas de dag gis smras pa/shes ldan dag ltos shig ngang pa gnyis kyis rus sbal zhig khyer te song ngo zhes zer ba na/rus sbal des kyang dang du blangs so//de nas ri brags gzhan zhig gi 
7b2('dul ba): steng na phar song ba dang /yang khye'u dang bu mo dag gis ma thong nas de dag gis smras ba/shes ldan dag ltos shing /ngang ba gnyis kyis rus sbal zhig/khyer te song ngo zhes zer pa dang /rus sbal des bsams pa/bdag gis yun ji srid du mtho 'tsham pa mnags par bya snyam nas/
7b3('dul ba): smras pa/ngang pa gnyis kyis kho bo khyer ba ma yin gyis 'di ltar kho bo rang 'gro'o zhes byas pa dang /shing shor nas de sa la lhang ste/de dag gis bzung nas tshul ma yin pas sdug bsngal bar byas pa dang lhas tshigs su bcad pa smras pa/mdza' bshes phan par 'dod
